# Current-preview control reconciliation

Every captured button,input,select,link and textarea is retained below. Text extraction confirms presence,not the actual click destination. Standard/source-derived behaviors are specified; unmatched destinations are explicitly open and block wiring. No control is connected to a convenient unrelated screen. Religious/demo/sample controls require the source decision.

This ledger does not contain an audit of undetected Figma click targets because no reactions were found in the inspected reference descendants. Original text layer names do not automatically identify interactive controls.
